- **scriptorium**: Turn a book (PDF, EPUB, DOCX, TXT, MOBI) into a chapter-by-chapter knowledge skill the agent can consult later at a fraction of the tokens: a lean SKILL.md with the author's frameworks, one file per chapter with page citations, glossary, patterns, a decision cheatsheet and, for theology books, a Scripture index. Use whenever the user wants to make a skill from a book, 'turn this book into a skill', 'transforme este livro em skill', 'book to skill', 'extract the method of this book', 'quero consultar este livro depois', or to update a book skill with a new edition or a second volume. Also use… ([victtordavi-spec/scriptorium](https://github.com/victtordavi-spec/scriptorium/tree/HEAD/scriptorium))
- **scriptorium-agents**: Full specification for all 12 agents in the scriptorium framework. Use when: asking what a specific agent does, writing a prompt to invoke an agent, configuring file pointers for an agent, understanding agent personas, asking about the Orchestrator/Architect/Researcher/Writer/ Reviewer roles, or troubleshooting unexpected agent output. Includes persona, model type, inputs, outputs, example invocation prompts, and key constraints for each agent. ([lordmos/scriptorium](https://github.com/lordmos/scriptorium/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/scriptorium-agents) · ★9)
- **scriptorium-epub-tools**: Reference guide for the EPUB build pipeline toolchain. Use when: understanding what external tools are needed, asking why Mermaid or code blocks are rendered the way they are, troubleshooting EPUB rendering issues, or deciding whether to install mmdc. Covers mmdc, Puppeteer, highlight.js, and Node.js built-ins — what each tool does, what problem it solves, and when it is required vs optional. ([lordmos/scriptorium](https://github.com/lordmos/scriptorium/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/scriptorium-epub-tools) · ★9)

- **scriptorium-workflow**: Step-by-step execution guide for the scriptorium 5-phase book production pipeline. - **scrna-seq-and-proteomics-matrix-loading**: Use when the task requires ingesting a cell-by-gene scRNA-seq matrix or an experiment-by-protein proteomics matrix (tsv/csv, hundreds of thousands of rows) as input for FAVA network inference or a PCC baseline on the same data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-and-proteomics-matrix-loading))
- **scrna-seq-anndata-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq matrices or downloaded dataset files (e.g. the 5800-cell Setty CD34+ bone-marrow data from the VIA Datasets repo) must be loaded as AnnData with PCA precomputed before running VIA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-anndata-preprocessing))
- **scrna-seq-annotation-confidence-thresholding**: Use when annotating scRNA-seq cells with a hierarchical classifier (e.g. scATOMIC run_scATOMIC() output) and you need per-class confidence cut-offs derived automatically from each class's score distribution rather than fixed thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-annotation-confidence-thresholding))
- **scrna-seq-annotation-error-detection**: Use when an annotated scRNA-seq AnnData (cell-type labels in adata.obs['label']) is suspected to contain mislabeled cells, and you want training-dynamics-based evidence plus marker-gene verification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrna-seq-annotation-error-detection))
- **scrna-seq-annotation-integration**: Use when a cancer scRNA-seq dataset (counts matrix, Seurat object, csv/rds/h5, or 10X folder) needs automated cell-type annotation within the tumor microenvironment and batch-effect-corrected integration before downstream analyses like inferCNV. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-annotation-integration))
- **scrna-seq-bam-barcode-splitting**: Use when input is one 10x Genomics barcoded BAM file (e.g., pbmc_8k) plus barcodes.tsv.gz and per-cell BAM files are required for downstream per-cell analyses such as geneBodyCoverage.pl. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-bam-barcode-splitting))
- **scrna-seq-batch-correction-embedding-inference**: Use when multiple scRNA-seq batches or studies (e.g., 9-batch pancreas atlas, immune or lung atlas datasets) need batch-corrected embeddings, or when new query datasets must be annotated against a pre-trained reference atlas without retraining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-batch-correction-embedding-inference))
- **scrna-seq-batch-effect-correction**: Use when a multi-batch scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., three cell lines across CEL-seq2, 10x Chromium, Drop-seq) has unknown cell types and batch effects must be corrected while clustering cells and benchmarking ARI against alternative integration methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-batch-effect-correction--ff1ce8cff1940e06))
- **scrna-seq-batch-effect-correction**: Use when a Scanpy AnnData object with raw scRNA-seq counts plus per-cell batch labels shows cells separating by batch rather than by cell type (e.g. skewed cell types across batches, nested batch effects, or &gt;10 batches), and an unsupervised integrated embedding is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-batch-effect-correction--0e353a3ffe052f95))
- **scrna-seq-batch-effect-simulation**: Use when you need a controlled scRNA-seq integration benchmark with simulated large batch effects, or to verify reported LB-scenario iLISI/50-RNN values for BATMAN, Seurat V3.0, MNNcorrect, and Scanorama. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-batch-effect-simulation))
- **scrna-seq-batch-integration-benchmarking**: Use when integrating multiple scRNA-seq batches or species (e.g., human–mouse atlas data up to ~1M cells) and you need to compare integration methods' batch mixing, cell-type conservation, and runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-batch-integration-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-benchmark-dataset-handling**: Use when you have a ranked DEG list from singleCellHaystack's `haystack` function (sorted by log.p.vals via show_result_haystack) and want to validate that top-ranked genes are enriched for known, high-confidence cell-type marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-benchmark-dataset-handling))
- **scrna-seq-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when testing whether engineered clustering features (e.g., Spearman-correlation features from SSCC) stay consistent with true cell-type labels across fluctuating subsampling rates of a benchmark scRNA-seq dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-benchmark-evaluation))
- **scrna-seq-cell-annotation**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix (rows = genes, columns = cells) and need per-cell activity scores of biological-process PPINs for cell annotation, UMAP embedding, or pseudotime inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-annotation))
- **scrna-seq-cell-cycle-effect-removal**: Use when scRNA-Seq expression data shows within-cell-type heterogeneity from the cell cycle that obscures cell-type differences, and a known set of cell-cycle genes (mouse or human) is available for control-gene annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-cycle-effect-removal))
- **scrna-seq-cell-embedding-extraction**: Use when trained cross-species cell-type classification models are available (e.g. TACTiCS from Zenodo) and you need per-cell embeddings from scRNA-seq data to assess cell-type alignment across species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-embedding-extraction))
- **scrna-seq-cell-label-annotation**: Use when you have a small selected marker-gene panel (e.g., a 15-marker panel) plus per-cell expression data and cell type labels, and you need to verify the panel preserves 13-way (or similar) cell type separation in a 2-D t-SNE embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-label-annotation))
- **scrna-seq-cell-type-aggregation**: Use when scRNA-seq expression with cell-type labels must be collapsed to per-cell-type profiles to score ligand-receptor interactions between sender and receiver cell types at a single time point. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-type-aggregation))
- **scrna-seq-cell-type-annotation**: Use when training scRNA-seq data with known cell labels exist and every test cell must receive exactly one of the K reference labels, especially with closely correlated cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-type-annotation))
- **scrna-seq-cell-type-classification-benchmarking**: Use when you need to compare malignant-vs-non-malignant cell prediction performance of an ensemble k-NN classifier against established cell-annotation tools on the same single-cell test datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-type-classification-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-cell-type-prediction**: Use when scRNA-seq cells must be labeled against the Cell Ontology controlled vocabulary, especially when the query or test set may contain cell type terms absent from the training labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cell-type-prediction))
- **scrna-seq-chemistry-configuration**: Use when FASTQ files from a UMI-based 3' scRNA-seq platform (e.g., Chromium, Drop-seq, ICELL8, SmartSeq3) must be processed with a single unified pipeline and the platform-specific chemistry is unknown or should be auto-detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-chemistry-configuration))
- **scrna-seq-cluster-annotation**: Use when scRNA-seq count matrices with pre-computed cluster assignments need automatic cell type labels, replacing subjective manual marker-gene annotation with a tissue-specific, evidence-scored reference lookup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cluster-annotation))
- **scrna-seq-cluster-annotation-criteria**: Use when annotating cell types for scRNA-seq clusters and marker genes must be identified by one-against-one paired cluster comparison with explicit fold-change, significance, and percent-expression thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cluster-annotation-criteria))
- **scrna-seq-cluster-assignment-analysis**: Decision support for whether visually distinct scRNA-seq clusters in a scDEED-optimized t-SNE embedding should be unified into one cluster — e.g. five Hydra neuron ectodermal subtypes or CAR-T clusters 3/4/9/11. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cluster-assignment-analysis))
- **scrna-seq-cluster-downsampling**: Use when you need to determine the minimum number of cells per cluster (here &gt;=20) at which automated cell-type annotations remain statistically indistinguishable from full-dataset annotations, or when auditing annotation robustness on sparse clusters of a Seurat object with a UMAP reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cluster-downsampling))
- **scrna-seq-cluster-label-consistency-evaluation**: Use when evaluating whether a cell-annotation tool's accuracy is limited by its bundled reference database, or when testing whether substituting an external taxonomy reference (e.g., CellMatch) improves cluster-label consistency on benchmark datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cluster-label-consistency-evaluation))
- **scrna-seq-cluster-marker-validation**: Use when a pretrained self-supervised scRNA-seq encoder (Miscell) has produced cell clusters and you need ranked, cluster-specific marker genes via attribution rather than differential-expression tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cluster-marker-validation))
- **scrna-seq-clustering**: Scaffolds a validated, config-driven Seurat scRNA-seq clustering pipeline (11-step Perturb-seq/CRISPR-screen variant, or a 10-step plain scRNA-seq variant) for a NEW dataset, porting the same steps validated in a prior production run. Use this whenever the user wants to set up, scaffold, or start a new single-cell RNA-seq clustering analysis, a Perturb-seq / CRISPR screen scRNA-seq pipeline, or mentions Seurat clustering, cell-type annotation, doublet detection, gRNA/guide assignment, MOI filtering, or SLURM scRNA-seq jobs for a new h5/CellRanger dataset — even if they don't use the word "ski… ([huangfulab/scrna-seq-clustering](https://github.com/huangfulab/scrna-seq-clustering/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **scrna-seq-clustering-based-deg-prediction**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., Tabula Muris bone marrow) needs cluster-based marker discovery, or when benchmarking a clustering-independent DEG method (singleCellHaystack) against the standard cluster-then-test Wilcoxon pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-clustering-based-deg-prediction))
- **scrna-seq-clustering-based-label-refinement**: Use when partial cell type annotations (e.g., scGate multi-classifier output) cover only a subset of cells in an integrated TIL map and complete labels are needed before a second semi-supervised STACAS integration round. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-clustering-based-label-refinement))
- **scrna-seq-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when comparing clustering methods on single-cell RNA-seq datasets with known cell-type labels, e.g. Baron, Zeisel, Zheng68K, or SC-Mixology, to decide which method performs . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrna-seq-clustering-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-clustering-evaluation**: Use when you need to evaluate scRNA-seq imputation/clustering models by clustering their learned cell embeddings and scoring agreement against true cell labels supplied in a h5 expression matrix (e.g., Adam, Romanov). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-clustering-evaluation--9ba94c14bd9e8022))
- **scrna-seq-clustering-evaluation**: Use when cell embeddings from a scRNA-seq integration or clustering model (AnnData with 'batch_indices' and 'cell_types' obs columns) must be quantitatively benchmarked via ARI/NMI clustering scores and kBET batch-mixing acceptance rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-clustering-evaluation--4b1aa77bb1a89be7))
- **scrna-seq-clustering-preprocessing**: Use when raw or unclustered scRNA-seq data (e.g., 10X Genomics counts from mouse skin wound samples GSM3453535-GSM3453538) must be QC-filtered and assigned to cell groups before ligand-receptor communication inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-clustering-preprocessing))
- **scrna-seq-cna-simulation**: Use when you need ground-truth CNA labels to benchmark copy-number callers on single-cell RNA-seq, by simulating known events (e.g. allele-specific chr3 loss) in a seeded tumor dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cna-simulation))
- **scrna-seq-communication-matrix-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq data (expression matrix plus cell-type labels) must be analyzed for intercellular communication, especially for non-human species or when results should not be constrained by known L-R databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-communication-matrix-inference))
- **scrna-seq-correlation-distance-computation**: Use when you have normalized scRNA-seq expression data with barcode lineage annotations (e.g., GEO GSE74923) and need to test whether related cells are transcriptionally more similar than random pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-correlation-distance-computation))
- **scrna-seq-count-aggregation-to-metacells**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset needs to be reduced from many individual cells to metacells (e.g. ~100-fold cell reduction on large atlases), or when metacell-level DE/DA/PCA must be weighted by metacell size to match single-cell reference results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-aggregation-to-metacells))
- **scrna-seq-count-data-simulation-with-batch-effects-and-dropout**: Use when validating a batch-effects correction or cell-type clustering method on scRNA-seq counts where ground-truth cell-type labels, known K, batch effects, and dropout rates must be controlled by simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-data-simulation-with-batch-effects-and-dropout))
- **scrna-seq-count-denoising**: Use when pre-training a source classification model on raw scRNA-seq count data where dropout events and overdispersion require a probabilistic denoising loss (ZINB negative log-likelihood) rather than mean-squared reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-denoising))
- **scrna-seq-count-denoising-with-zinb-autoencoder**: Use when a sparse, overdispersed scRNA-seq count matrix shows dropout and downstream tasks (DE, visualization, clustering) suffer from noise, and counts are the input (not TPM/CPM). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-denoising-with-zinb-autoencoder))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-downsampling**: Use when stress-testing the robustness of a single-cell integration or label-transfer pipeline against simulated dropout noise in scRNA-seq counts, at graded dropout rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-downsampling--01fad6b442c90f86))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-downsampling**: Use when you must quantify a method's scalability on large scRNA-seq data by regressing runtime against number of cells on a series of downsampled count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-downsampling--a9332a22d92127fb))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-handling**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. from GEO, ArrayExpress, GSA, Tabula Muris, PBMC SCP424, GSE131907, GSE116470) must be filtered, normalized, and split into train/test matrices with labels for cell-type identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-handling))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq expression matrix (genes × cells) must be normalized and filtered before feature selection or clustering, following the scEFSC protocol. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-preprocessing--59b331a6c988eb1e))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when the input is a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (cell x gene) that must be read into a Seurat/Scanpy-compatible object before rare cell detection with GapClust or marker validation with Seurat FindMarkers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-preprocessing--2ba004bc7621e671))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices must be quality-controlled and prepared as anndata objects before mapping new cells (e.g. Gfi1−/−, Irf8−/−, double-KO GMPs) onto an existing STREAM reference graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-preprocessing--6c933a316ae693e3))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-processing**: Use when scRNA-seq data from 10x Chromium 3' (v2) or 5' (v1) Gene Expression workflows must be converted into per-cell UMI count matrices and per-cell mutation genotype calls (from _counts_UB.tsv or cb_sniffer/VarTrix output) to connect genotype to phenotype at single-cell resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-processing--7522f2313c5729b6))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-processing**: Use when starting from a raw unfiltered gene-by-barcode count matrix (e.g. Read10X_h5 h5 or mtx) that must be transposed, cell-called (EmptyNN, threshold 100), and processed in Seurat before clustering or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-processing--c6af41ce00cf308a))
- **scrna-seq-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when you need to assess how dropout prevalence affects an imputation method's performance — e.g., benchmarking scImpute on simulated count matrices with known cell subpopulations and DE gene ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-count-matrix-simulation))
- **scrna-seq-count-simulation**: Use when a pipeline needs a ground-truth simulated scRNA-seq count matrix (known DE vs non-DE genes, zero inflation, within-sample correlation) to validate gene-level or gene-set testing methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/scrna-seq-count-simulation))
- **scrna-seq-cross-platform-classification**: Use when a scRNA-seq reference dataset must label cells from a different platform (Smart-seq2 vs 10x) or condition (normal vs tumor), and method performance (accuracy, F1-score, sensitivity/specificity, unlabeling rate) must be compared. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-cross-platform-classification))
- **scrna-seq-data-clustering**: Use when clustering a large scRNA-seq expression matrix (e.g. tens of thousands of cells, such as the Pollen dataset or 68,578-cell blood cell dataset) and standard k-means or PCA-based clustering is too slow or inaccurate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-data-clustering))
- **scrna-seq-data-imputation**: Use when a normalized, log1p-transformed scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. Klein dataset, top 5000 HVGs) contains dropouts and a denoised, complete cell-by-gene expression matrix is needed for downstream clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-data-imputation))
- **scrna-seq-dataset-acquisition-from-geo**: Use when a benchmark task requires the seven annotated real scRNA-seq datasets referenced by GEO accessions GSE84133 (Baron pancreas) and GSE65525 (Klein mouse ES) plus human PBMC, kidney, hypothalamus, mouse kidney, and turtle brain, each with ground-truth cell-type labels for ARI computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dataset-acquisition-from-geo))
- **scrna-seq-dataset-acquisition-geo**: Use when a reproduction or benchmark needs the exact processed single-cell RNA-seq data and precomputed UMAP/tSNE embeddings cited in a paper, and the paper's data-availability section names a GEO accession (e.g. GSE87038, GSE129788). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dataset-acquisition-geo))
- **scrna-seq-dataset-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking how a single-cell method's CPU time and memory scale with dataset size, requiring size-stratified subsets drawn from a large atlas such as MOCA (10k to 900k cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dataset-downsampling))
- **scrna-seq-dataset-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data must be prepared for deep clustering with scBGEDA, requiring gene filtering and selection of highly variable genes before running SDDA.py and BC.m. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dataset-preprocessing--944f604badd24729))
- **scrna-seq-dataset-preprocessing**: Use when raw or published scRNA-seq count/TPM matrices must be normalized before clustering: values exceed 100, low-level expressed genes are present, and downstream feature selection needs a comparable scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dataset-preprocessing--f4c991aed50e023f))
- **scrna-seq-dataset-subsampling**: Use when benchmarking rare-cell or doublet detection methods on scRNA-seq data and controlled, labeled datasets with known rare-cell abundances are needed as inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dataset-subsampling))
- **scrna-seq-demultiplexing**: Use when pooled scRNA-seq libraries were labeled with hashtag oligonucleotides and each droplet in an (HTOs x droplets) HTO count matrix must be assigned to its sample of origin (including multiplet detection), with uncertain droplets discarded via error probabilities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-demultiplexing))
- **scrna-seq-denoising-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a CITE-seq or multimodal dataset provides protein-level sub-population labels (e.g., CD56bright/CD56dim NK cells from CD16/CD56 ADTs) and you must quantify whether denoised RNA better matches that protein structure than raw counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-denoising-benchmark-evaluation))
- **scrna-seq-deseq-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., mESC-SMARTer / ola_mES_2i.RData) must be normalized and log-transformed to log2(TPM+1) before reCAT clustering, scoring, or marker-gene distribution plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-deseq-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-developmental-trajectory-analysis**: Use when analyzing how L-R pair expression changes across ordered developmental time points (e.g., GSE153164 corticogenesis) and you need to assign pairs to temporal clusters and identify peak time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-developmental-trajectory-analysis))
- **scrna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a cell-by-gene expression matrix has cells labeled by clone/copy-state (e.g., XClone Clone_ID annotation) and the goal is to test whether DEGs between clones are enriched in LOH regions, as in TNBC1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-differential-expression-analysis))
- **scrna-seq-differential-expression-testing**: Use when scRNA-seq expression data comes with predicted cluster labels (e.g. from scBGEDA on datasets like QS_Heart) and the goal is to assign cell-type identities by testing differential expression per cluster against published marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-differential-expression-testing))
- **scrna-seq-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a log2-transformed scRNA-seq matrix (cells x genes) and need a 2D embedding that reveals rare cell populations more cleanly than t-SNE or UMAP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dimensionality-reduction))
- **scrna-seq-discriminative-gene-set-selection**: Use when many phenotype groups (e.g., cell-type/tissue combinations) have overlapping DE genes and per-group markers fail, and a smallest global feature set distinguishing every phenotype pair is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-discriminative-gene-set-selection))
- **scrna-seq-disease-condition-annotation-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq AnnData object is annotated by coarse experimental condition (disease vs healthy) and you want to test whether DNN training-dynamics confidence tracks graded disease severity via marker module expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrna-seq-disease-condition-annotation-analysis))
- **scrna-seq-downsampling-stratified-by-stage**: Use when benchmarking cell-cycle pseudo-time methods for robustness on scRNA-seq data, specifically to test whether model-based (Cyclum) vs model-free (reCAT) approaches degrade with fewer cells or genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-downsampling-stratified-by-stage))
- **scrna-seq-dropout-imputation-autoencoder**: Use when a sparse scRNA-seq count matrix (h5/csv/tsv, &gt;80-95% zeros) needs dropout imputation before downstream analyses such as clustering, cell-type separation, differential expression, or MSE benchmarking against other imputers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dropout-imputation-autoencoder))
- **scrna-seq-dropout-imputation-benchmarking**: Use when a QC-filtered scRNA-seq count matrix has high dropout (40%-90% missing values) and the goal is to compare imputation methods' downstream effects on clustering, visualization, DE analysis, or pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dropout-imputation-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-dropout-rate-analysis**: Use when selecting between NB and ZINB noise models for DCA denoising of an scRNA-seq count matrix, i.e., when you must determine whether the observed zeros are fully explained by the mean-dropout relationship or require an explicit zero-inflation component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-dropout-rate-analysis))
- **scrna-seq-embedding-generation**: Use when a sxRNA-seq dataset contains multiple batches/samples (batch.var present) and the goal is a batch-corrected embedding for joint clustering, cell-type annotation, or benchmarking against integration methods such as scVI or Harmony. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-embedding-generation))
- **scrna-seq-expression-matrix-construction**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data must be turned into an R gene expression matrix as input to SSCC/sscClust subsampling-based clustering of large-scale datasets (e.g. 68k PBMC-scale). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-matrix-construction))
- **scrna-seq-expression-matrix-loading**: Use when the input is a .mat expression matrix from the SERM benchmark data (e.g. data_observed_10perc.mat with 12162 cells x 2422 genes) and it must be loaded into a Python array before imputation or timing benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-matrix-loading--a205efb5b1f617d8))
- **scrna-seq-expression-matrix-loading**: Use when raw scRNA-seq counts stored in an h5 file (with true labels) must be loaded via the scGCL --dataset argument and pre-processed with the &gt;1% non-zero QC filter, size-factor log-normalization, and top-2048 HVG selection before graph construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-matrix-loading--a2c07d879318f201))
- **scrna-seq-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when you have a normalized (TPM/CPM or Seurat NormalizeData()) scRNA-seq matrix — genes as rows, cells as columns — that must be matched to the PreCanCell DEG panel and scaled before malignant/non-malignant cell classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-matrix-preprocessing))
- **scrna-seq-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix and want per-cell activity of BP-associated PPINs or a BP x cell activity matrix for downstream correlation/annotation analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-matrix-processing--26f1c4bc896fe604))
- **scrna-seq-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when input is a raw scRNA-Seq count/expression matrix with cell-type or malignant/non-malignant annotations and the goal is per-cell signalling entropy estimation of differentiation potency or cancer stem-cell phenotypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-matrix-processing--607b096dba7a9541))
- **scrna-seq-expression-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. Wang NPC 483-cell differentiation or Yang E10/E17 hepatoblast datasets) need normalized expression profiles before per-cell network or entropy computations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-expression-profile-analysis**: Use when you have a precomputed neighbour-distance matrix from a large scRNA-seq dataset and need to estimate the size k of a potential rare cell cluster from distance-derivative statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-expression-profile-analysis))
- **scrna-seq-feature-interpretation**: Use when interpreting which genes drive scRNA-seq classification: after training tree-based models on CKG and gene-expression matrices, to test whether top-10 CKG features map to the same genes as top-10 GE features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-feature-interpretation))
- **scrna-seq-feature-selection-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a feature selection method improves single-cell clustering accuracy versus baselines, or when quantifying runtime/memory scaling on downsamplings up to million-cell scRNA-seq datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-feature-selection-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-fragment-size-modeling**: Use when paired-end scRNA-seq fragment lengths are available and you need a reusable empirical fragment length distribution (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-fragment-size-modeling))
- **scrna-seq-functional-network-inference**: Use when building or benchmarking protein functional association networks from sparse, redundant scRNA-seq or proteomics expression matrices (tsv/csv), especially when simple co-expression correlation underperforms or the dataset exceeds what tools like scLink can process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-functional-network-inference))
- **scrna-seq-fusion-candidate-statistical-testing**: Use when a candidate fusion feature table with per-cell supporting chimeric-read counts, partner-gene expression covariates, and GC-content values must be tested against a background-noise distribution to assign p-values and FDR estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-fusion-candidate-statistical-testing))
- **scrna-seq-fusion-gene-detection**: Use when running targeted fusion-gene detection on paired-end scRNA-seq FASTQs from cancer datasets (e.g., K562 Smart-seq2 or multiple myeloma t(4;14) cohorts) and comparing sensitivity against STAR-Fusion, FusionCatcher, Arriba, FusionInspector, or scFusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-fusion-gene-detection))
- **scrna-seq-gene-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when the input is a normalized single-cell gene expression matrix (genes × cells) and the goal is direct gene–gene association networks per cell, overcoming CSN's overestimation of indirect associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-gene-expression-matrix-processing))
- **scrna-seq-gene-expression-statistics**: Use when you have a raw gene-by-cell count matrix (e.g. GEMLI_example_data_matrix.RData) and need to reproduce or audit how GEMLI selects candidate gene-expression-memory genes via mean expression and CV2 variability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-gene-expression-statistics))
- **scrna-seq-gene-filtering**: Use when scRNA-seq counts from multiple subjects (e.g., iPSC dopaminergic differentiation time points) must be converted into per-donor gene co-expression adjacency matrices as input for MuDCoD or similar personalized dynamic community detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-gene-filtering))
- **scrna-seq-gene-program-scoring**: Use when a preprocessed single-cell expression matrix must be reduced to per-cell scores for named gene sets (e.g. AXL-high vs MITF-high resistance programs) and correlated to test program relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-gene-program-scoring))
- **scrna-seq-generative-model-benchmarking**: Decision support for choosing a synthetic scRNA-seq generator: use when multiple simulation tools can be trained on the same .h5/.h5ad single-cell count matrix and you need quantitative fidelity (MMD) and realism (RF-AUC) rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-generative-model-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-ground-truth-label-construction**: Use when real per-individual UMI count matrices (e.g., 10x Chromium PBMC, mouse lung, skin) lack definitive cell-type labels and you need marker-derived approximated ground truth to benchmark clustering via adjusted Rand index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-ground-truth-label-construction))
- **scrna-seq-gse-power-simulation**: Use when benchmarking GSE method power on simulated DE summary statistics (beta, beta_var), e.g. to quantify how much more iDEA is than fGSEA, CAMERA, PAGE or GSEA at fixed FDR in scRNA-seq settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-gse-power-simulation))
- **scrna-seq-h5-dataset-loading**: Use when your analysis starts from an h5 file containing an scRNA-seq expression matrix, true labels, and other information (e.g. the adam Alzheimer's dataset feeding scGCL), and you need it parsed into a cell-by-gene matrix plus reference labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-h5-dataset-loading))
- **scrna-seq-h5ad-data-loading**: Use when the input is a single-cell RNA-seq dataset in .h5ad format that must be read with Scanpy and preprocessed (HVG selection + PCA into obsm['X_pca']) before DV computation or sketching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-h5ad-data-loading))
- **scrna-seq-heterogeneity-scenario-design**: Use when benchmarking multi-individual scRNA-seq clustering methods (e.g. BAMM-SC vs SIMLR, SC3, DIMM-SC) and you need factorial simulation grids varying heterogeneity among individuals and cohort size, with ARI as the outcome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-heterogeneity-scenario-design))
- **scrna-seq-imputation-ablation-with-saver**: Use when you need to quantify how MUSIC's SAVER-based Data_imputation and Cell_filtering (cell_num_threshold=20) preprocessing steps change the overall gene perturbation effect ranking, e.g. to benchmark preprocessing sensitivity of a CROP-seq/Perturb-seq analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-imputation-ablation-with-saver))
- **scrna-seq-imputation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether an scRNA-Seq imputation method recovers cell-type structure or differential expression signal from dropout/NB-noise matrices, against AutoClass and control methods on known-truth data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-imputation-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-joint-clustering-across-batches**: Use when multiple sxRNA-seq batches/samples must be jointly clustered with batch effects removed while retaining interpretable gene-module factors, e.g. to annotate cell types or compare integration performance against scVI, Harmony, or LIGER. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-joint-clustering-across-batches))
- **scrna-seq-label-one-hot-initialization**: Use when a pre-processed source scRNA-Seq matrix X_src comes with ground-truth cluster labels y_src and those annotations should steer NMF dictionary learning before transferring knowledge to an unlabeled target dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-label-one-hot-initialization))
- **scrna-seq-label-prediction-with-rejection**: Use when annotating scRNA-seq datasets from different labs or protocols using labeled reference datasets, especially when annotations are at different resolutions and unknown cell populations must be rejected or discovered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-label-prediction-with-rejection))
- **scrna-seq-latent-feature-extraction**: Use when scRNA-seq data contain dropout events and you need a nonlinear low-dimensional (2D) cell representation for visualization, lineage re-establishment, or downstream gene-marker discovery via correlation with latent features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-latent-feature-extraction))
- **scrna-seq-latent-feature-learning**: Use when scRNA-seq expression matrices (e.g., .npz of HVG features, AnnData) need cell-type-invariant latent representations for clustering, and deep generative baselines like scVI are too costly or hard to optimize. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-latent-feature-learning))
- **scrna-seq-latent-representation-scoring**: Use when target scRNA-seq latent representations have confirmed bimodal E-score distributions (via bimodality coefficient or Hartigan's dip test) and cells must be split into known vs unknown groups without a manual threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-latent-representation-scoring))
- **scrna-seq-library-depth-normalization**: Use when preparing raw count matrices from scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq datasets (e.g. 10X neuron_9k and nuclei_900) as input to scID or similar methods that require library-depth-normalized, non-log-transformed data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-library-depth-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-library-size-normalization-and-rescaling**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI count matrices (e.g. Tabula Muris Spleen-10X_P4_7 ExpressionData.csv) must be normalized and rescaled to the (0,1) interval before feeding scRNA_seq_preprocessing output into scGATE_edge()/scGATE_logic() network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-library-size-normalization-and-rescaling))
- **scrna-seq-library-size-normalization-log-transform**: Use when starting from raw cell-by-gene FACS scRNA-seq counts (Tabula Muris Figshare CSVs) that must be filtered, normalized by library size, log-transformed, and reduced to 50 principal components before embedding evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-library-size-normalization-log-transform))
- **scrna-seq-lineage-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq data lacks lineage labels but you need cell lineage relationships, e.g., to study heritable gene expression, symmetric vs asymmetric cell fate decisions, or DEGs at cell fate switches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-lineage-inference))
- **scrna-seq-log-l2-normalization**: Use when preprocessing raw scRNA-seq count matrices (CSV or JLD2 with rows=cells, columns=genes) before dimensionality reduction, especially for sparse data where conventional log normalization would distort signals via unequal cell vector lengths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-log-l2-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-log-normalization-preprocessing**: Use when raw SeqFISH/scRNA-seq count matrices must be converted to AnnData h5ad with truth locations in .obs[['x','y']] and log-normalized (scran) before contrastive model training or spatial reconstruction benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-log-normalization-preprocessing))
- **scrna-seq-log-transformation-and-gene-centering**: Use when a raw scRNA-Seq count matrix (genes × cells) is about to be fed into ccRemover or PCA-based factor analysis, and the paper's protocol requires log-scaling and gene-wise centering first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-log-transformation-and-gene-centering))
- **scrna-seq-log2-variance-scaling-preprocessing**: Use when preparing scRNA-seq count matrices (mESC, melanoma Mel78-88, hESC 10X Chromium) as input to Cyclum's circular-manifold autoencoder or comparators like reCAT, Seurat, or ccRemover. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-log2-variance-scaling-preprocessing))
- **scrna-seq-malignant-cell-classification**: Use when annotating patient-derived cancer scRNA-seq or spatial transcriptomics data to label malignant cells within an already-identified cancerous lineage and to obtain a continuous malignancy probability per cell or spot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-malignant-cell-classification))
- **scrna-seq-malignant-vs-epithelial-deg-testing**: Use when building malignant-vs-normal cell classifier features from a signature-calibrated training set and you need per-dataset DEG testing (Seurat FindAllMarkers) followed by a cross-dataset union. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-malignant-vs-epithelial-deg-testing))
- **scrna-seq-marker-gene-cluster-assignment-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantify whether a clustering solution (e.g., scDCC with pairwise constraints vs. unconstrained) improves marker-gene cluster specificity in an scRNA-seq dataset, via paired per-gene specificity scores and a one-sided Wilcoxon test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-marker-gene-cluster-assignment-evaluation))
- **scrna-seq-marker-projection**: Use when you have scRNA-seq data with cellular labels and a selected marker-gene panel (e.g., scGeneFit's 15-marker CBMC panel) and need to verify whether the reduced marker space preserves cell type distinctions via t-SNE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-marker-projection))
- **scrna-seq-measurement-noise-estimation**: Use when fitting CTMM to pseudobulk scRNA-seq (OP or CTP) and per-observation measurement noise variances (nu, ctnu) must be supplied as model inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-measurement-noise-estimation))
- **scrna-seq-memory-bank-management**: Use when adapting a pre-trained source classifier to target scRNA-seq cells without source data and you need globally retrieved, weighted neighbors (bidirectional/unidirectional/extensional) as self-supervised affinity constraints. - **scrna-seq-method-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating which automatic scRNA-seq cell-type identification method (eager, lazy, or marker-based supervised) performs on a manually annotated dataset, measured by accuracy, F1-score, unlabeling rate and speed. - **scrna-seq-minmax-cell-normalization**: Use when a raw gene-by-cell scRNA-seq count matrix is about to be fed into scDHA's non-negative kernel autoencoder or any model that expects per-cell expression profiles scaled to [0, 1] with non-negative values. - **scrna-seq-mirna-activity-inference**: Use when you need to predict miRNA expression or activity from scRNA-seq clusters or bulk RNA-seq profiles lacking miRNA measurements, e.g. Seurat objects from HCL, TCGA, CCLE, or induced-miRNA experiments. - **scrna-seq-multi-study-dataset-integration**: Use when building a reference T cell atlas (e.g., murine TIL or LCMV viral CD8 T cell atlas) from multiple public scRNA-seq studies whose samples, protocols, and batches differ and must be aligned before annotation or query projection. - **scrna-seq-network-activity-scoring**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix (genes as rows, cells as columns) and need per-cell activity scores for biological processes such as cell differentiation, proliferation, or immune response. - **scrna-seq-noise-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a scRNA-Seq denoising/imputation method against controls: you need a noisy count matrix with known ground-truth expression and cell labels, or a real dataset (e.g., Tian et al. 305-cell mixture) degraded with dropout or negative binomial noise. - **scrna-seq-normalization-hvg-selection**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., from Tabula Muris, 'Qx Limb Muscle', or PDAC datasets) must be normalized and reduced to highly variable genes prior to training a graph convolutional autoencoder such as scMGCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-normalization-hvg-selection))
- **scrna-seq-novel-cell-type-detection**: Use when annotating target scRNA-seq data under open or open-partial settings where target-private cell types may exist, raw source data are unavailable, and no prior threshold for 'unknown' detection is known. - **scrna-seq-outlier-cell-detection**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (genes × cells) is being imputed with scImpute and aberrant cells should be flagged and excluded before dropout imputation. - **scrna-seq-pca-latent-space-benchmarking**: Use when comparing two versions or parameterizations of a DEG/differentially-active-feature method across many scRNA-seq atlas datasets (e.g. 32 Tabula Muris + 87 Mouse Cell Atlas) to quantify rank agreement, runtime savings, and discrepant genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-pca-latent-space-benchmarking))
- **scrna-seq-per-cell-quality-metric-computation**: Use when tall-format (melted) scRNA-seq expression data has been loaded as a Spark DataFrame and per-cell/per-gene quality summaries are needed before applying data_filter() thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-per-cell-quality-metric-computation))
- **scrna-seq-per-gene-dropout-summarization**: Use when you need per-gene quality metrics (cells expressing &gt; 0, dropout count/percentage, mean/total expression) from tall-format scRNA-seq data to drive gene-level filtering in a Spark-based pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-per-gene-dropout-summarization))
- **scrna-seq-pipeline**: Scanpy-based single-cell RNA-seq analysis pipeline covering loading (10X, h5ad), QC, normalization, HVG selection, PCA/UMAP, Leiden clustering, differential expression, and batch correction (Harmony, scVI). - **scrna-seq-population-downsampling**: Use when designing a benchmark where a cell population is artificially sparsified to test whether trajectory or clustering outcomes depend on sampling density, before attempting generative restoration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-population-downsampling))
- **scrna-seq-prediction-probability-extraction**: Use when a trained TOSICA Transformer model has annotated query scRNA-seq cells (e.g., monocyte subtypes in pan-cancer tumor-infiltrating myeloid data) and per-cell Prediction, Probability, and gene-set attention values must be exported for validation or enrichment testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-prediction-probability-extraction))
- **scrna-seq-preprocessing-and-filtering**: Use when an scRNA-seq expression matrix (gene x cell) is about to be imputed (e.g., with scISR) or k-means clustered and contains zero-only genes, sparsely expressed genes (&lt;2 cells, &lt;1% non-zero entries), or value ranges exceeding 100. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-preprocessing-and-filtering))
- **scrna-seq-preprocessing-normalization**: Use when raw single-cell or spatial transcriptomic count matrices must be prepared before inferring neuron-neuron communication networks with NeuronChat, or before benchmarking inferred connectivity against anatomical tracing data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-preprocessing-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-preprocessing-normalization-scaling**: Use when raw single-cell gene expression count matrices (e.g. AnnData/scRNA-seq data) must be filtered, normalized, log-transformed and scaled prior to clustering methods such as scAce that require pre-processed input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-preprocessing-normalization-scaling))
- **scrna-seq-preprocessing-scanpy**: Use when a raw or minimally processed scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. Veres et al. stage-5 beta-like differentiation, 25,299 cells) must be turned into a cell-type-annotated adata object before running FateCompass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-preprocessing-scanpy))
- **scrna-seq-preprocessing-sctransform**: Use when the input is a raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrix (e.g. from NGDC HRA000084 or GEO GSE84133) that must be preprocessed before building a cell-to-cell KNN graph and training a graph autoencoder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-preprocessing-sctransform))
- **scrna-seq-preprocessing-workflow**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads (10X-style droplet data) must be aligned and converted to gene-by-cell UMI count matrices and then quality filtered non-arbitrarily, especially when spliced/unspliced matrices are needed for RNA velocity or trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-preprocessing-workflow))
- **scrna-seq-pseudoalignment-quantification**: Use when raw scRNA-seq FASTQ files from a 3'-end tag chemistry (e.g. 10xv2/10xv3) must be converted to gene- and UTR isoform-level count matrices without whole-transcript alignment. - **scrna-seq-qc**: Process, quality-control, annotate, and visualize single-cell or single-nucleus RNA-seq datasets across tissues and species. Use when Codex needs to build, adapt, or review a general scRNA-seq QC pipeline; choose dataset-appropriate cell-level filters from QC distributions; run required scDblFinder-based doublet and ambient-RNA filtering; annotate cells with matched references or marker-based fallbacks; or generate global and per-group UMAP visualizations for large scRNA-seq datasets. ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ngs-analysis/skills/scrna-seq-qc) · ★8k · 22 copies)
- **scrna-seq-qc-filtering-and-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq count matrices (e.g. filtered_feature_bc_matrix.h5 plus ATAC fragments .tsv.gz) must be QC-filtered and normalized in R/Seurat/Signac before multimodal integration or pairing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-qc-filtering-and-normalization))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control**: Use when reprocessing 10x Genomics 3'-end scRNA-seq FASTQs with scUTRquant and you must verify that gene counts from a truncated UTRome reference remain concordant with Cell Ranger before trusting downstream UTR-isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control-and-imputation**: Use when input is a raw gene-by-cell expression profile plus per-cell perturbation annotations (e.g. crop_stimulated or 10X perturb_GSM2396857 format) that must be QC'd and imputed before MUSIC topic modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control-and-imputation))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (10x-formatted mtx directory or CSV) must be filtered for cells with &lt;200 expressed genes, genes in &lt;3 cells, and high mitochondrial fractions before normalization and clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering--4efa74601c8e028c))
- **scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when starting from raw single-cell count matrices (e.g. .h5ad from GEO deposits like GSE149614 or Figshare-hosted atlas datasets) that need cleaning before batch correction, clustering, or differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-quality-control-filtering--3e6f71a0adb901d0))
- **scrna-seq-quality-matrix-construction**: Use when building the compact percentile-coverage matrix from SkewC gene body coverage output (coverage.r) as input for SkewC plotting, trim clustering, and typical-vs-skewed cell classification. - **scrna-seq-rare-cell-scoring**: Use when the goal is to detect rare cell populations in a scRNA-seq count matrix (thousands to ~68k+ profiles) and no annotated clusters exist yet; FiRE gives a per-cell continuous rareness score in seconds where RaceID/GiniClust scale poorly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-rare-cell-scoring))
- **scrna-seq-read-pseudoalignment**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads (e.g., 10x Genomics v3 FASTQ, such as sample GSM4626009 from GSE152766) need fast alignment and quantification into spliced and unspliced count matrices for downstream quality filtering with COPILOT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-read-pseudoalignment))
- **scrna-seq-run-statistics-collection**: Use when a scKB pseudoalignment run on 10xv3 FASTQ data (e.g. the 80 MB toy_data from GSM4626009, GSE152766) has finished and you must verify and gather its 8 output files for downstream COPILOT input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-run-statistics-collection))
- **scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing scalability of clustering or cluster-merging methods on scRNA-seq data: you need wall-clock runtime on a fixed, small, cell-type-labelled subset to contrast with AUROC performance on the full dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking--bce9883289b40420))
- **scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the runtime and memory footprint of pluripotency/scRNA-seq scoring tools (ORIGINS, LandSCENT SR, CytoTRACE) on GEO count matrices (breast GSE113197, colon GSE125970, hematopoietic GSE117498, lung GSE161383). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-runtime-benchmarking--983ec1f0f6552bba))
- **scrna-seq-self-supervised-representation-learning**: Use when you need to cluster or embed a large scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. tens of thousands of cells, like the 43,095-cell 10x PBMC dataset) and standard pipelines underperform, or to benchmark deep-learning representation learning against Scanpy/Seurat/scVI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-self-supervised-representation-learning))
- **scrna-seq-similarity-learning**: Use when clustering scRNA-seq data (cells x genes matrix with ground-truth labels available for evaluation) and noisy/high-dropout expression makes naive cell-to-cell similarity unreliable; need NMI/ARI gains from similarity learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-similarity-learning))
- **scrna-seq-sparsity-comparison-across-technologies**: Use when comparing synthetic-data quality from small pilot sub-samples between scRNA-seq technologies (10x Genomics vs Smart-seq2), specifically to quantify whether sparsity degrades small-to-large inference via DBI, ARI, and cluster-proportion metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-sparsity-comparison-across-technologies))
- **scrna-seq-spatial-mapping**: Use when you need to link scRNA-seq cells to spatial coordinates/positions via optimal transport — e.g., mapping 1297 Drosophila embryo cells onto 3039 seqFISH/in situ spatial positions from geometry.txt and dge_normalized.txt/bdtnp.txt inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-spatial-mapping))
- **scrna-seq-spectral-clustering-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix with known cell-subpopulation labels (e.g., embryonic stages) is available and you must quantify whether imputation improves cell-population clustering accuracy against no-imputation and competitor imputers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-spectral-clustering-accuracy-evaluation))
- **scrna-seq-splicing-status-prediction**: Use when scRNA-seq reads labeled 'ambiguous' (compatible with both spliced and unspliced transcripts) need a probabilistic spliced/unspliced resolution, e.g. PBMC1k-style reads aligned to a spliceu augmented reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-splicing-status-prediction))
- **scrna-seq-stage-wise-dice-dynamics**: Use when a time-course or staged single-cell RNA-Seq dataset spans a known or suspected lineage bifurcation (e.g., retinal progenitors splitting into neuronal vs epithelial fates) and the exact transition/tipping stage must be resolved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-stage-wise-dice-dynamics))
- **scrna-seq-stemness-scoring**: Use when a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq expression matrix must be ranked by pluripotency/stemness to identify stem or progenitor cells, e.g. to pick trajectory-inference origins or to check whether a potency method recovers a known cellular hierarchy (HSC &gt; MPP &gt; GMP, stem vs TA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-stemness-scoring))
- **scrna-seq-stratified-cross-validation**: Use when benchmarking supervised cell-type identification methods on labeled scRNA-seq datasets and a fair, repeated train/test comparison of accuracy across datasets is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-stratified-cross-validation))
- **scrna-seq-study-data-preparation**: Use when importing new scRNA-seq (RNA-, ATAC-, or CITE-seq) studies into Cellenium, i.e. when raw/normalized single-cell data must be converted to annotated H5AD files with semantically organized study metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-study-data-preparation))
- **scrna-seq-subclone-clustering-tsne-visualization**: Use when deposited scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., drug-sensitive vs. resistant HMCL pairs like U266P vs U266VR) must be clustered into subclones and screened for drug-target gene enrichment to prioritize combination therapies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-subclone-clustering-tsne-visualization))
- **scrna-seq-subset-sampling**: Use when benchmarking the wall-clock runtime of scRNA-seq imputation methods (ALRA, MAGIC, DCA, scImpute, SAVER) as a function of cell count, requiring random subsets of a large filtered expression matrix.
