- **scrna-seq-technology-parameter-extraction**: Use when building a reference table of supported single-cell RNA-seq chemistries and their barcode/UMI read-layout settings, or when verifying which technology a FASTQ input corresponds to. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-technology-parameter-extraction))
- **scrna-seq-temporal-data-handling**: Use when you have metabolic labeling scRNA-seq data (e.g., scEU-seq rpeLabeling.h5ad with separate labeled, unlabeled, and total count matrices plus a labeling-time vector) and need paired time-point inputs for RNAForecaster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-temporal-data-handling))
- **scrna-seq-to-bulk-3prime-seq-concordance**: Use when scUTRquant 3'UTR isoform quantification from scRNA-seq must be validated against bulk 3'-seq reference TPMs (e.g., ArrayExpress E-MTAB-7391 HSC or PolyASite-annotated ESC bulk data) to establish concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-to-bulk-3prime-seq-concordance))
- **scrna-seq-to-scatac-seq-label-transfer**: Use when integrating unpaired scRNA-seq (annotated source, h5ad with obs['CellType']) and scATAC-seq (unlabeled target) data to transfer cell-type labels, e.g. SNARE-seq or mouse-atlas benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-to-scatac-seq-label-transfer))
- **scrna-seq-train-test-split-preparation**: Use when a labeled cancer scRNA-seq dataset must be partitioned into training and testing sets to benchmark cell-type or cell-subtype annotation tools on the same cells, e.g., before computing per-cluster accuracy/F1 against original study annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-train-test-split-preparation))
- **scrna-seq-training-dataset-construction**: Use when building a supervised malignant-vs-normal epithelial training set from labeled scRNA-seq datasets where the raw 'Malignant'/'Tumor' annotations may be noisy and need refinement via cancer signature activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-training-dataset-construction))
- **scrna-seq-uncertainty-measurement**: Use when a pre-trained source classifier's soft probability outputs exist for scRNA-seq target cells and you need a per-cell uncertainty score to detect which cells belong to known cell types versus novel types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-uncertainty-measurement))
- **scrna-seq-variational-autoencoder-training**: Use when a UMI count matrix (cells x genes) needs low-dimensional embedding for visualization, batch correction across multiple batch factors (e.g., 30 patients x 3 health statuses x 2 locations), or mapping of held-out individuals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-variational-autoencoder-training))
- **scrna-seq-yan-dataset-processing**: Use when a Yan (or similar scRNA-seq) expression matrix and SSRE-inferred cluster labels are available and the goal is validating that the clustering supports downstream DEG detection via Kruskal-Wallis testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-yan-dataset-processing))
- **scrna-seq-zero-entry-partitioning**: Use when preprocessing a sparse scRNA-seq count matrix before imputation and you need to decide, per zero entry, whether it is a dropout event or a true zero rather than imputing all zeros indiscriminately. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-seq-zero-entry-partitioning))
- **scrna-seurat-basics**: Use when the user references `生信教程` 第9-11课附近的单细胞基础、10x 读取、`CreateSeuratObject`、课堂 R/Seurat 入门和最基本对象创建流程。 ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/scrna-seurat-basics))
- **scrna-significant-violin-plots**: Seurat and ggplot2 violin-plot significance annotation for single-cell expression comparisons. Use when adding per-cell-type group comparisons to VlnPlot or geom_violin outputs, extracting gene expression from Seurat objects, running Wilcoxon tests with BH correction, placing significance stars above violins, exporting per-gene PDF or CSV outputs, or adapting classroom or pasted scRNA visualization snippets into reusable code. ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/scrna-significant-violin-plots))
- **scrna-stage-aggregation-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq AnnData object already contains per-cell TEI (or similar gene age-weighted index) in obs and the goal is to summarize and plot mean index per developmental stage to detect an hourglass pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrna-stage-aggregation-analysis))
- **scrna-trajectory-inference**: Reconstruct differentiation trajectories from scRNA-seq using PAGA, diffusion pseudotime, scVelo RNA velocity, and CellRank fate probabilities. Use when ordering cells along a developmental axis, identifying branch points, or computing cell-fate probabilities. ([UViiVe/fleet-skills-public](https://github.com/UViiVe/fleet-skills-public/tree/HEAD/scrna-trajectory-inference))
- **scrna-trajectory-monocle**: Use when the user references `生信教程` 第17-19课的 `monocle2`、`monocle3`、伪时序、轨迹重建或从 Seurat 对象转到 Monocle 分析的流程。 ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/scrna-trajectory-monocle))
- **scrna-treatment-response**: Compare immune-cell compositions between response groups using patient-level aggregation, deterministic inference, and explicit longitudinal-label safeguards. ([sagirimo/BioDSH](https://github.com/sagirimo/BioDSH/tree/HEAD/biodsh-core/skills/scrna-treatment-response) · ★69)
- **scrna-virtual-knockout**: In-silico single-gene knockout on scRNA-seq (scTenifoldKnk-style): build a gene regulatory network, virtually knock out a gene, and rank the genes most affected by its loss. ([sagirimo/BioDSH](https://github.com/sagirimo/BioDSH/tree/HEAD/biodsh-core/skills/scrna-virtual-knockout) · ★69)
- **scrna_celltype_annotation**: Assigns cell type labels to single-cell RNA-seq data using a multi-method annotation approach with self-correction, including mLLMCelltype, manual marker gene validation, and hierarchical sub-clustering. ([Nyha15/BioAnalyze](https://github.com/Nyha15/BioAnalyze/tree/HEAD/backend/skills/scrna_celltype_annotation))
- **scrna_differential_abundance**: Performs differential abundance (DA) testing on scRNA-seq data to identify cell types or states whose proportions shift across experimental conditions. Does it by celltype. Supports simple two-group comparisons. ([Nyha15/BioAnalyze](https://github.com/Nyha15/BioAnalyze/tree/HEAD/backend/skills/scrna_differential_abundance))
- **scrna_differential_gene_expression**: Performs differential gene expression (DGE) analysis on scRNA-seqseq data using pseudobulk aggregation and statistical modeling. Emphasizes proper model specification with biological and technical covariates, contrast definitions, and multiple testing correction. Supports simple two-group comparisons, multi-group contrasts, interaction models, and continuous covariate designs. ([Nyha15/BioAnalyze](https://github.com/Nyha15/BioAnalyze/tree/HEAD/backend/skills/scrna_differential_gene_expression) · 2 copies)
- **scrnametaboliclandscape**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/scrnametaboliclandscape) · ★745)
- **scrnametaboliclandscape**: Comprehensive metabolic landscape analysis pipeline for scRNA-seq data. This is an all-in-one process group performing complete metabolic pathway analysis including expression imputation, feature selection, pathway activity calculation, and heterogeneity analysis. Based on methodology from Xiao et al. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-ml/scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2) · ★30 · 7 copies)

([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2) · ★745)
- **scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2f974fd5**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/scrnametaboliclandscape-pwwang-immunopipe-2f974fd5) · ★745)
- **scrnaseq-accession-data-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark requires the original Yan, Goolam, or Deng embryo scRNA-seq count matrices and only a GEO/ArrayExpress accession (e.g. GSE36552) is known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-accession-data-retrieval))
- **scrnaseq-alignment-with-star**: Use when input is paired Smart-Seq single-cell RNA-seq data named *_1.fastq/*_2.fastq and a genome reference (.fa) plus GTF are available, and per-cell alignments are needed for fusion or splice-aware analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-alignment-with-star))
- **scrnaseq-annadata-construction**: Use when raw scRNA-seq studies must be combined into a single AnnData with explicit batch (source study) and cell-type annotation columns before training scETM or running batch-integration benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-annadata-construction))
- **scrnaseq-annotation-audit**: Audit cell type annotations against h5ad expression data. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-annotation-audit))
- **scrnaseq-auto-annotation**: Use when running scRNAseq.py auto mode for LLM annotation. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-auto-annotation))
- **scrnaseq-batch-effect-quantification**: Use when comparing batch-integration/dimension-reduction methods (e.g., corralm, corralm+Harmony, corralm+MNN, multibatch PCA, Seurat) on multi-batch scRNA-seq count matrices and you need paired metrics of cell-type separation (ASW_celltype) vs batch mixing (1-ASWbatch). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-batch-effect-quantification))
- **scrnaseq-batch-integration-mixing-evaluation**: Use when two scRNA-seq datasets from different species (e.g., human and mouse primary motor cortex raw counts) must be integrated into one latent space and you need a mixing metric plus cross-species cell-type matching recall to judge embedding quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-batch-integration-mixing-evaluation))
- **scrnaseq-batch-integration-model-training**: Use when you have raw count matrices from multiple scRNA-seq batches (e.g. .h5ad AnnData, ≥2 batches, up to ~1M cells) and need an unsupervised integrated embedding plus an ablation-based attribution of which adversarial components drive batch correction vs bio-conservation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-batch-integration-model-training))
- **scrnaseq-bifurcation-trajectory-benchmarking**: Use when comparing accuracy of pseudotime inferred by bifurcation-capable trajectory methods on a single-cell RNA-seq differentiation dataset, using marker genes as an external pseudotime reference (e.g. Pearson/Spearman/Kendall correlation with ENO3, MEF2C, MYH2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-bifurcation-trajectory-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-cell-annotation**: Cell type annotation for scRNA-seq in Seurat 5: manual marker-gene inspection and automated reference-based annotation with SingleR/celldex. Use when annotating clusters, identifying cell types, running SingleR, choosing a reference, or interpreting marker gene / module score results. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-cell-annotation) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-cell-cycle-pseudo-time-recovery**: Use when you have unsynchronized scRNA-seq expression data and need to order cells along the cell cycle (G1/S/G2-M) or associate another single-cell modality (e.g., bisulfite methylation) with a recovered cell cycle pseudo time-series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-cycle-pseudo-time-recovery))
- **scrnaseq-cell-group-label-permutation-simulation**: Use when running NeuronChat (or its shared CellChat/CellPhoneDB workflow) on scRNA-seq or spatial transcriptomics and you must decide which inferred ligand-receptor communication links are statistically significant, or when testing p-value stability across permutation counts (M=100 vs M=1000). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-group-label-permutation-simulation))
- **scrnaseq-cell-subsampling-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when you must determine the minimum cell count needed for reliable logic-gate/GRN inference from scRNA-seq, or characterize how inference quality and runtime saturate with sample size and candidate TF-set size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-subsampling-sensitivity-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-cell-type-count-aggregation**: Use when you have cell-type-annotated scRNA-seq data across multiple samples/conditions and need to build a cell count matrix (e.g., via sccoda.util.cell_composition_data.from_pandas or from_scanpy) as input for compositional differential abundance analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-type-count-aggregation))
- **scrnaseq-cell-type-label-assessment**: Use when you need to quantitatively judge how well a dimension-reduction/visualization method separates known cell-type labels in scRNA-seq data, e.g. benchmarking an embedding against PCA, t-SNE, UMAP, or SCANPY. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cell-type-label-assessment))
- **scrnaseq-cluster-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing automatic cell type annotation of scRNA-seq clusters (scCATCH vs Seurat-based one-against-all markers), especially to quantify pseudo marker gene effects on annotation consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cluster-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-cluster-stability-assessment**: Use when scRNAseq clusters must be validated for reconstruction robustness — e.g. after clustering with an SCA-encoded space (TF/miRNA/kinase targets) on datasets like setA, or when comparing a variant latent space (e.g. vSCA) against a baseline SCA run via QCM/QCC trajectory superimposability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cluster-stability-assessment))
- **scrnaseq-clustering**: Graph-based clustering for scRNA-seq in Seurat 5: FindNeighbors, FindClusters, resolution selection and clustree stability checks. Use when clustering cells, choosing a clustering resolution, building an SNN/KNN graph, or asking what the "right" number of clusters is. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-clustering) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-clustering-ensemble-implementation**: Use when multiple scRNAseq clustering results must be integrated into one consensus cell-assignment set via average similarity on binary matrices, with silhouette-guided k selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrnaseq-clustering-ensemble-implementation))
- **scrnaseq-clustering-quality-diagnosis**: Use when scRNA-seq Leiden clustering looks wrong. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/scrnaseq-clustering-quality-diagnosis))
- **scrnaseq-compositional-analysis**: scRNA-seq DA via scCODA for cell-type proportions. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-compositional-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-condition-wise-subsetting**: Use when a pooled single-condition-agnostic scRNA-seq matrix with cell-type annotations and patient/condition labels must be divided into separate disease-state groups before per-group ligand-receptor inference (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-condition-wise-subsetting))
- **scrnaseq-confounder-corrected-pca**: Use when preprocessing 10X scRNA-seq matrices (matrix.mtx/gene.tsv/barcode.tsv) prior to clustering, to correct confounders like cell cycle or mitochondrial load and to choose the number of PCs via shuffled-data simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-confounder-corrected-pca))
- **scrnaseq-confounding-factor-correction**: Use when a scRNAseq expression matrix shows unwanted variation (e.g. ERCC spike-in confounding or cell-cycle effects) and downstream clustering must recover known biological groupings from corrected data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-confounding-factor-correction))
- **scrnaseq-count-aggregation-by-cell-type**: Use when a scRNA-seq anndata (.h5ad) matrix with per-cell metadata (cell_type, donor_id, condition) must be reduced to pseudo-bulk counts per sample and cell type before calling DEGs between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-aggregation-by-cell-type))
- **scrnaseq-count-data-modeling**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices need a denoised, low-dimensional latent feature representation that explicitly models zero inflation (dropout) before downstream cell-type clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-data-modeling))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-loading**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices must be ingested into R before SCEVAN analysis — e.g., GSE131928 GBM samples (MGH102, MGH104, MGH105, MGH106) or multiple myeloma 10X data destined for pipelineCNA or multiSampleComparisonClonalCN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-loading))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-melting**: Use when a wide-format scRNA-seq count matrix (.csv) with one column per gene must be reshaped into a tall cell-gene-value table before Parquet serialization and downstream scSPARKL analysis on low-memory hardware. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-melting))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization**: Use when a raw or unnormalized scRNA-seq count matrix from two biological conditions (e.g. treated vs untreated cells) must be normalized in Seurat before calling differentially expressed genes for downstream regulatory-element queries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization**: Use when you have a per-cell sparse k-mer abundance matrix (from jellyfish counts) and a pre-built CKG look-up table, and need reference-free CKG-level cell features for downstream cell type classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-normalization))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when input is a raw scRNA-Seq count matrix (gene x cell CSV) that must be quality-filtered and reduced to highly variable genes before imputation or K-means clustering evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--fb45dcfeca37a037))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw unfiltered droplet-based scRNA-seq count matrix (.h5 or .mtx) must be shaped for EmptyNN cell-calling or PU-learning based filtering of cell-free droplets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--10b88a3414841bc2))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (e.g. GEO accession like GSE81682 mouse HSPC) must be normalized and converted with cellexalvrR into CellexalVR-consumable input files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--b5ce2bfe69570a2e))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix is sparse and dropout-prone and must be filtered, HVG-selected, and normalized to [0,1] before binarization or landmark construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing--6ae154ee4e887cb1))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-processing**: Use when two cross-species scRNA-seq datasets (count matrices) must be preprocessed and paired with a gene similarity distance matrix as input to a cell type matching model such as TACTiCS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-processing))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when you need a simulated scRNA-seq benchmark dataset with known cell-type labels and controllable cluster sizes (e.g., a rare population &lt;5% of cells) to evaluate clustering accuracy and robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-simulation--6f66c5e05e736952))
- **scrnaseq-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when you need a simulated scRNA-seq gene-by-cell count matrix to validate penalized Latent Dirichlet Allocation (pLDA) topic modeling, or to sanity-check topic-word probability estimation on data with known latent structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-matrix-simulation--6c2a33f24c96c6a1))
- **scrnaseq-count-simulation**: Use when an evaluation needs synthetic scRNA-Seq count data with known ground-truth cell-cluster labels — e.g. benchmarking clustering or transfer-learning pipelines where ARI can only be computed if true labels exist. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-simulation--8de9e1e51e73bf7d))
- **scrnaseq-count-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a cell-cycle or latent-factor removal method on scRNA-Seq data where ground-truth effect assignments (which PCs are cell-cycle, which genes are cell-type) must be known exactly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-count-simulation--ed455474c2d999ae))
- **scrnaseq-cross-group-ppt**: Use for scRNAseq cross-group PPTs with DA/DEG/GO/TE data. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-cross-group-ppt))
- **scrnaseq-cross-platform-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when several labeled scRNA-seq studies of the same tissue exist and you need to quantify how annotation accuracy transfers between studies/platforms (inter-study annotation) across competing classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-cross-platform-benchmark-evaluation))
- **scrnaseq-data-formatting**: Use when raw scRNA-seq data (e.g. GEO accession GSE116753 or similar) must be reshaped into MDIC3's exp.txt/metadata.txt input format before GRN inference with GNIPLR and communication scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-data-formatting))
- **scrnaseq-data-input-format-preparation**: Use when scRNA-seq data with cell-type and time-point metadata must be formatted into the expression/cell-type/time-point files that dsCellNet requires before running datainput and downstream network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-data-input-format-preparation))
- **scrnaseq-dataset-harmonization**: Use when several scRNA-seq datasets with expression matrices, dataset-of-origin labels, and cell-type labels must be combined to test whether cell types (e.g. neurons vs non-neuronal cells) replicate across studies via AUROC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-dataset-harmonization))
- **scrnaseq-dataset-retrieval-geo-scp**: Use when a benchmark or method comparison requires raw scRNA-seq count matrices with ground-truth cell labels and the paper lists source repositories (GEO/SCP accession numbers, e.g. Table 1 of the scDHA article) to fetch them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-dataset-retrieval-geo-scp))
- **scrnaseq-dataset-subsampling**: Use when you must test whether a scRNAseq clustering method's performance (e.g., ARI on Zheng68K) is robust to dataset size and cell-type complexity, by benchmarking on controlled subsampled datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrnaseq-dataset-subsampling))
- **scrnaseq-dataset-subsetting**: Use when benchmarking PAS/scoring tools on million-cell-level scRNA-seq data where full-dataset computation is impractical, and a fixed-size random cell subset (e.g., 100k cells from the &gt;1.2M-cell lupus atlas) suffices for validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-dataset-subsetting))
- **scrnaseq-dataset-subsetting-and-downsampling**: Use when a large single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g. a whole-tumor immune atlas) must be narrowed to a biologically relevant population (immune cells) and a fixed number of cells (e.g. 5000) before per-cell scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-dataset-subsetting-and-downsampling))
- **scrnaseq-deep-learning**: Use scVI, scANVI, totalVI, scGPT, Geneformer, and scFoundation for single-cell RNA-seq analysis. Use for batch correction, label transfer, perturbation prediction, and foundation model fine-tuning. Covers training, inference, GPU sizing, and evaluation metrics. ([pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills](https://github.com/pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/machine-learning-bio/scrnaseq-deep-learning) · ★8)
- **scrnaseq-differential-expression**: Differential gene expression for scRNA-seq in Seurat 5: per-cluster marker discovery (FindAllMarkers/FindMarkers, Wilcoxon) and cross-condition testing via pseudobulk aggregation with edgeR/limma. Use when asked to find marker genes, compare gene expression between conditions/groups/patients, run FindMarkers or FindAllMarkers, or interpret a log fold-change or p-value from a single-cell experiment. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-differential-expression) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-dimensionality-reduction**: PCA, t-SNE and UMAP for scRNA-seq in Seurat 5: choosing the number of PCs, running RunPCA/RunTSNE/RunUMAP, and interpreting the output correctly. Use when visualizing single-cell data in 2D/3D, picking dims for UMAP, interpreting PCA loadings, or explaining what a UMAP/t-SNE plot does and doesn't show. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-dimensionality-reduction) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-doublet-detection-ensemble-analysis**: Use when processing droplet-based scRNA-seq data (Seurat/SingleCellExperiment objects) that lacks experimental doublet labels (no cell hashing, MULTI-seq, or demuxlet), and doublets must be called computationally per sample before downstream clustering or trajectory analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-doublet-detection-ensemble-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-dropout-masking**: Use when benchmarking gene-imputation methods on scRNA-seq count matrices (h5 format) under controlled synthetic dropout, e.g. to verify a method recovers masked non-zero entries better than baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-dropout-masking))
- **scrnaseq-dropout-masking-simulation**: Use when benchmarking an scRNA-seq imputation method on controlled dropout recovery — i.e., you have complete pre-processed count matrices and need to quantify NMSE/RMSE/MAE recovery at artificially masked non-zero values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-dropout-masking-simulation))
- **scrnaseq-embedding-suitability-evaluation**: Use when fitting trajectories (e.g., Slingshot, Monocle3) on scRNA-seq data and you must decide among candidate embeddings from feature selection (number of highly variable genes) and dimension reduction choices, or need to know if a trajectory is appropriate at all. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-embedding-suitability-evaluation))
- **scrnaseq-enrichment-analysis**: Gene set enrichment analysis for scRNA-seq DE results: over-representation analysis (ORA, clusterProfiler::enrichGO/enricher) and GSEA. Use when running enrichment analysis, interpreting GO/KEGG/MSigDB/Hallmark results, choosing an ORA universe/background, or preparing a ranked gene list for GSEA. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-enrichment-analysis) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-entropy-model-implementation**: Use when implementing or validating an entropy-based cluster purity score for scRNA-seq clusters, i.e. computing single-gene sample entropy, LOESS-derived ds residuals, and aggregating them into ROGUE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-entropy-model-implementation))
- **scrnaseq-expression-matrix-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices for a labeled training set and a testing set (e.g., ASD snRNA-seq patients vs controls) must be normalized and restricted to their shared genes prior to annotation or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-expression-matrix-normalization))
- **scrnaseq-expression-matrix-permutation**: Use when you must verify that a matrix-imputation pipeline (e.g., SERM) is invariant to the arrangement of cells and genes — i.e., row/column permutations of a scRNA-seq count matrix yield near-identical Pearson correlations to the reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-expression-matrix-permutation))
- **scrnaseq-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when a single-cell scRNA-seq expression matrix (anndata object) and Step 1 cell phase posteriors are available and genes not yet labeled cyclers must be screened for de novo circadian rhythmicity before being added to the current cycler set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-expression-matrix-processing))
- **scrnaseq-gene-specificity-screen**: Determine whether a set of genes is specifically highly expressed in particular cell types or tissues using the Human Protein Atlas (HPA) public API. Downloads the RNA expression matrices for 154 cell types (nCPM) and 51 tissues (nTPM), screens for genes whose peak falls inside a user-defined cell-type group (such as the megakaryocytic lineage or hematopoietic cells), and outputs per-cell-type / per-tissue bar charts plus Excel/TSV results. Use it to judge the cell-type or tissue specificity of a gene list, to annotate any gene list or cNMF/GEP gene module for expression specificity, or to fi… ([Sculptor815/scrnaseq-gene-specificity-screen](https://github.com/Sculptor815/scrnaseq-gene-specificity-screen/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **scrnaseq-half-sample-resampling**: Use when comparing marker-selection methods (e.g. G-PC, CEM-PC, mRMR, ReliefF, F values) on scRNA-seq expression matrices and you need to quantify how reproducible each method's selected feature sets are across repeated random resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-half-sample-resampling))
- **scrnaseq-homogeneity-detection**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset is about to undergo trajectory/pseudotime inference and you must first verify it actually contains a continuous trajectory (i.e., is not homogeneous cells or disconnected clusters). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-homogeneity-detection))
- **scrnaseq-imputation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing imputation methods on scRNA-seq count matrices to test whether imputation improves cell-type separation or preserves biological zeros, e.g. before-vs-after classification error on downsampled Zeisel/Baron/Chen/La Manno data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-imputation-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-integration**: Multi-sample / multi-batch integration for 10x Genomics scRNA-seq in Seurat 5, using layer-based CCA (IntegrateLayers) on the RNA or SCT assay. Use when samples cluster by donor/batch/technology instead of by cell type on a UMAP/t-SNE, when asked to "correct for batch effects", "integrate samples", or "remove batch effect", or before joint clustering of multiple samples. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-integration) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-latent-space-construction-from-target-sets**: Use when a scRNAseq counts table with cell-cluster assignments (e.g. setA, clusters 1-5) must be functionally annotated by reconstructing clusters in an encoded space derived from validated target/signature sets rather than gene expression alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-latent-space-construction-from-target-sets))
- **scrnaseq-library-size-offset-normalization**: Use when analyzing scRNA-seq count matrices (UMI or read counts) across cells with varying sequencing depth, and a Tweedie/GLM-style model requires a library-size offset log(S) instead of pre-normalizing counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-library-size-offset-normalization))
- **scrnaseq-log-normalized-matrix-handling**: Use when feeding raw perturbation (Y) and control (Z) cell-by-gene count matrices into scDecouple or similar scCRISPR-seq pipelines that require log-normalized, HVG-subtracted matrices as input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-log-normalized-matrix-handling))
- **scrnaseq-log2-transformation-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (rows = cells, columns = genes) must be pre-processed and log2-transformed before dropout imputation or before measuring K-means cell-clustering accuracy with ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-log2-transformation-preprocessing))
- **scrnaseq-marker-gene-identification**: Use when clustering a scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., Chen, Xin, Gierahn benchmarks) requires per-cluster marker genes with explicit fold-change, significance, and expression-prevalence cutoffs to drive automatic cell-type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-marker-gene-identification))
- **scrnaseq-marker-selection-benchmarking**: Use when comparing marker-selection methods on scRNA-seq phenotype-classification tasks: you have count matrices with cell-type/phenotype labels and need discriminative marker sets plus downstream classifier F1 scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-marker-selection-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-mixed-transcriptional-profile-simulation**: Use when a paired scRNA-seq and spatial transcriptomics dataset is available and cell type proportions must be simulated with known ground truth to train or benchmark a deconvolution model (e.g., SpatialDDLS on 10x Visium/Chromium data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-mixed-transcriptional-profile-simulation))
- **scrnaseq-multi-resolution-louvain-clustering**: Use when an scRNA-seq dataset must be clustered and there is no principled resolution choice: cluster across a resolution grid, compute marker specificity per configuration, and pick the resolution maximizing the specificity index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-multi-resolution-louvain-clustering))
- **scrnaseq-neighborhood-scale-analysis**: Use when selecting a t-SNE perplexity/kEff for single-cell embeddings, or when characterizing whether scRNA-seq data have one or multiple intrinsic neighborhood scales via per-sample EMBEDR p-value sweeps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-neighborhood-scale-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-normalization-scaling**: Normalization (LogNormalize), variable feature selection and scaling for scRNA-seq in Seurat 5, plus SCTransform as an alternative. Use when normalizing counts, running NormalizeData/FindVariableFeatures/ScaleData or SCTransform, selecting the number of variable features, or preparing a QC-filtered object for PCA. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-normalization-scaling) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-normalized-expression-processing**: Use when constructing ligand-receptor communication edges from a normalised single-cell expression matrix and need an explicit, reproducible definition of gene 'expression' per cell type before edge extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-normalized-expression-processing))
- **scrnaseq-normalized-matrix-qc-processing**: Use when the input is a barcoded scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. GEO accession GSE99915) needing a QC-filtered, normalized expression matrix plus cell-ID-to-barcode-ID lineage annotations before lineage inference or memory-gene analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-normalized-matrix-qc-processing))
- **scrnaseq-pipeline**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/scrnaseq-pipeline) · ★745)
- **scrnaseq-pipeline**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Workflow_Management/workflows/scrnaseq-pipeline) · ★7)
- **scrnaseq-pipeline-gptomics-bioskills-2**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/scrnaseq-pipeline-gptomics-bioskills-2) · ★745)
- **scrnaseq-post-clustering-qc**: Post-clustering QC: filter cells, detect doublets. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/scrnaseq-post-clustering-qc))
- **scrnaseq-preprocessing-quality-control**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data must be filtered (min/max genes per cell, min cells per gene, mitochondrial percentage, HVG dispersion) before single-cell drug-response prediction with scDEAL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-preprocessing-quality-control))
- **scrnaseq-principal-component-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether denoising or imputation of scRNA-seq data preserves biological structure, by comparing principal component subspaces of original vs. perturbed/imputed matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-principal-component-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-pseudotime-gene-expression-modeling**: Use when a sample-level trajectory (PILOT diffusion-map embedding + EIPLGraph pseudotime, e.g. from myocardial_infarction.h5ad) already exists and you must rank cell populations changing along progression and identify cluster-specific genes via Wald tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-pseudotime-gene-expression-modeling))
- **scrnaseq-qc-filtering**: Quality control and cell filtering for 10x Genomics scRNA-seq in Seurat 5. Use when loading CellRanger output, computing per-cell QC metrics (mitochondrial/ribosomal/haemoglobin fraction, nFeature, nCount), choosing filtering thresholds, or removing low-quality cells and doublets. Covers human, mouse and Drosophila. From the SIB single-cell transcriptomics course. ([sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training](https://github.com/sib-swiss/single-cell-r-training/tree/HEAD/llm-packs/scrnaseq-qc-filtering) · ★9)
- **scrnaseq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when preprocessing 10X Genomics scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., GEO samples GSM3453537/GSM3453538) before building a CellChat object, and cells must be filtered by UMI count, gene count, and mitochondrial fraction thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-quality-control-filtering--5719d268f788c843))
- **scrnaseq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when a raw counts matrix or Seurat object (csv/rds/h5 or 10X folder) enters a scRNA-seq workflow and cells must be filtered for quality before annotation, clustering, or gene-set scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-quality-control-filtering--54323f323dcf3556))
- **scrnaseq-random-subsampling-pilot-design**: Use when planning scRNA-seq experiments from small pilot data: you need to test whether generative models trained on sub-sampled pilot datasets can produce synthetic data whose clustering, cell-type proportions, and per-gene distributions match a larger ground-truth dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-random-subsampling-pilot-design))
- **scrnaseq-read-alignment-star**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads (.sra/.fastq, e.g., from the Kim dataset) must be mapped to HUMAN reference (read_length 100) to produce BAMs feeding featureCounts and GATK-based SNV calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-read-alignment-star))
- **scrnaseq-read-simulation**: Use when a study needs synthetic scRNA-seq reads (BAM/FASTQ) that mimic real data at both read-sequence and read-count levels, e.g., benchmarking UMI deduplication tools or comparing read-coverage fidelity against simulators like minnow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-read-simulation))
- **scrnaseq-reference-based-deconvolution**: Use when spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., Visium spots with multi-cell content, or Slide-seq singlet/doublet spots) must be assigned cell-type labels using a paired, cell-type-annotated scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq reference, and accuracy must be benchmarked per slice vs. jointly across slices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-reference-based-deconvolution))
- **scrnaseq-reference-based-proportion-estimation**: Use when you have bulk RNA-seq read counts from a complex tissue and a matching scRNA-seq reference with cell type annotations, and need cell type proportion estimates without pre-selected marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-reference-based-proportion-estimation))
- **scrnaseq-reference-based-spot-deconvolution**: Use when a seq-based ST dataset (e.g., 10X Visium spots) needs per-spot cell type proportions estimated from a matched scRNA-seq reference, or when cell-type composition must be obtained before Splane spatial-domain identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-reference-based-spot-deconvolution))
- **scrnaseq-reference-curation**: Use when deciding whether an available scRNA-seq reference (e.g., one missing key cell types like neurons, or an atlas from a different tissue such as mouse brain) is fit for reference-based deconvolution of multi-cellular pixel-resolution ST data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-reference-curation))
- **scrnaseq-reference-mapping**: Use when a query scRNA-seq (or spatial MERFISH) dataset must be annotated by transferring labels from a matched reference scRNA-seq atlas sharing the same tissue, especially when batch effects separate the two datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-reference-mapping))
- **scrnaseq-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing computational scalability of rare-cell identification methods on scRNA-seq data, or when judging whether a method can process datasets in the tens-of-thousands-of-cells range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-runtime-benchmarking))
- **scrnaseq-scanpy-core-analysis**: End-to-end scRNA-seq analysis in Python using Scanpy and the scverse stack — QC, normalization, integration, clustering, annotation, pseudobulk DE. Use when analyzing 10X / Drop-seq / Smart-seq data and you want a Python (not R) workflow. ([UViiVe/fleet-skills-public](https://github.com/UViiVe/fleet-skills-public/tree/HEAD/scrnaseq-scanpy-core-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-seurat-core-analysis**: End-to-end scRNA-seq analysis in R using Seurat v5 — QC, SCTransform, Harmony / CCA / RPCA integration, clustering, annotation, pseudobulk DE. Use when working in R/Bioconductor and the upstream stack is already Seurat-based. ([UViiVe/fleet-skills-public](https://github.com/UViiVe/fleet-skills-public/tree/HEAD/scrnaseq-seurat-core-analysis))
- **scrnaseq-similarity-matrix-visualization**: Use when you need to visually compare cell-type or developmental-stage separation produced by different learned cell-to-cell similarity matrices on scRNA-seq data, e.g. benchmarking SSRE against Corr, SIMLR, MPSSC, SSR on the Goolam or Yan datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-similarity-matrix-visualization))
- **scrnaseq-simulation-with-ground-truth-subclusters**: Use when validating or benchmarking a cluster-merging / cluster-significance method (e.g. Cytocipher cluster-merge) on simulated scRNA-seq data where the true subcluster structure is known and an AUROC against ground-truth labels is the target metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-simulation-with-ground-truth-subclusters))
- **scrnaseq-srt-cross-platform-comparison**: Use when a benchmark produces per-method accuracy scores computed separately on SRT and scRNA-seq reference datasets (e.g., accuracy_scores.csv) and you need to test cross-platform agreement of method rankings/scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/scrnaseq-srt-cross-platform-comparison))
- **scrnaseq-summary-statistics-construction**: Use when scRNA-seq count data are available and gene-level DE effect sizes (beta coefficients and their variances) must be generated as input for iDEA's joint DE and gene set enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-summary-statistics-construction))
- **scrnaseq-time-series-trajectory-inference**: Use when a large single-cell RNA-seq dataset with collection time-point labels needs a balanced subsample before trajectory inference, especially to exclude cells whose pseudotime disagrees with their collection time point. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-time-series-trajectory-inference))
- **scrnaseq-transfer-learning-clustering**: Use when clustering a small scRNA-Seq target dataset (e.g. rare cell types, 300–600 cells) for which a large, well-annotated source dataset from a related tissue or context exists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-transfer-learning-clustering))
- **scrnaseq-trimean-expression-aggregation**: Use when preparing per-cell-group average expression of ligands, receptors, and cofactors from scRNA-seq data before inferring cell-cell communication probabilities with CellChat or a reimplementation of its mass-action model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-trimean-expression-aggregation))
- **scrnaseq-wasserstein-distance-computation**: Use when you have a multi-sample scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. patient biopsies with cell-type annotations, PCA embedding, patient and disease-status columns) and need sample-level distances to detect disease-associated patient subgroups without patient labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/scrnaseq-wasserstein-distance-computation))
- **scrobble**: Scrobble music to Last.fm. Use when user wants to record/scrobble album plays, individual tracks, or NTS radio shows. Handles named albums (looks up tracklist), provided tracklists, and NTS radio show format (auto-detects by timestamp patterns like 0:00:10 and show metadata). Reports success after scrobbling.
